- **molecular-dynamics-openmm**: 当需要对蛋白质/小分子体系跑经典分子动力学（MD）模拟并做轨迹分析时使用；用 OpenMM 搭体系、跑能量最小化与 NVT/NPT 平衡及产能模拟，再用 MDAnalysis 算 RMSD/RMSF/接触/自由能并出图；不适用于量子化学/电子结构（DFT）、分子对接打分（docking）或纯实验数据统计制图；触发词：分子动力学、MD模拟、OpenMM、MDAnalysis、RMSD、RMSF、力场、PDBFixer、molecular dynamics、trajectory analysis ([findscripter/everything-skills](https://github.com/findscripter/everything-skills/tree/HEAD/09-verticals/molecular-dynamics-openmm) · ★11 · 2 copies)
- **molecular-dynamics-rmsd**: Calculate RMSD between reference and target atomic coordinate sets for molecular dynamics validation. ([XuShuwenn/RedAct](https://github.com/XuShuwenn/RedAct/tree/HEAD/data/skills/trace2skill/skills_raw/combined/molecular-dynamics-rmsd) · ★13 · 4 copies)
- **molecular-dynamics-setup**: Classical molecular dynamics set-up and analysis: force-field and ML-potential choice, units, time step from the fastest mode, constraints and HMR, thermostats/barostats that sample the right ensemble, PME, the equilibration protocol, NVE drift checks, and RDF/MSD/Green-Kubo observables with OpenMM, LAMMPS, GROMACS or ASE. Load when building, running or reviewing an MD simulation. ([rustcircle/c101](https://github.com/rustcircle/c101/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/molecular-dynamics-setup))
- **molecular-dynamics-simulation**: 当需要对蛋白质/小分子体系做分子动力学（MD）模拟与轨迹分析时使用；用 OpenMM 搭建体系并跑能量最小化、NVT/NPT 平衡与产能模拟，再用 MDAnalysis 算 RMSD/RMSF/接触/自由能并出图；不适用于量子化学/电子结构计算、对接打分（docking）或纯实验数据统计；触发词：分子动力学、MD模拟、OpenMM、MDAnalysis、RMSD、RMSF、力场、蛋白质动力学、molecular dynamics、trajectory analysis、PDBFixer ([findscripter/everything-skills](https://github.com/findscripter/everything-skills/tree/HEAD/09-verticals/molecular-dynamics-simulation) · ★11 · 2 copies)
- **molecular-dynamics-simulation**: Use when a task involves packing solvent boxes, preparing biomolecular or small-molecule MD inputs, running GROMACS or OpenMM, or checking minimization, equilibration, and trajectory analysis. ([yyywaa/Cloudra-mechanism-agent](https://github.com/yyywaa/Cloudra-mechanism-agent/tree/HEAD/cloudra/skills/molecular-dynamics-simulation) · ★11)
- **molecular-dynamics-simulation**: Use when setting up, running, or reviewing a classical molecular dynamics simulation (GROMACS, NAMD, OpenMM, or AMBER). ([serbulent-av/agentic-coding](https://github.com/serbulent-av/agentic-coding/tree/HEAD/skills/molecular-dynamics-simulation))
- **molecular-dynamics-simulation-review**: Use when a task involves reviewing a molecular dynamics simulation plan or result for system preparation, force field, equilibration, and sampling adequacy to identify the research or operational question, target population or system, relevant version, sensitive data, and approval boundary before acting. Use current primary sources, produce a traceable artifact, and verify it against explicit criteria. Trigger for planning, analysis, review, or troubleshooting in this focused domain; do not execute external writes or clinical actions without authorization. ([Manoj-11-Dahal/try-Skills](https://github.com/Manoj-11-Dahal/try-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/science-research/life-sciences/molecular-dynamics-simulation-review) · ★3)
- **molecular-ecologist**: Think and work like an expert Molecular Ecologist. Use when a task calls for Molecular Ecologist judgment. Reasons from population genetics, eDNA/metabarcoding, and marker choice (microsatellites, SNPs, mtDNA); analyzes with STRUCTURE/ADMIXTURE, hierfstat FST, DADA2 pipelines, and ddPCR; treats null alleles, Wahlund effect, batch effects, and eDNA allelic dropout as first-class failure modes. ([K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents](https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents/tree/HEAD/scientific-agents/molecular-ecologist/skills/molecular-ecologist) · ★255 · 2 copies)
- **molecular-ecologist**: Expert-thinking profile for Molecular Ecologist (field / lab / computational population & environmental genetics): Reasons from population genetics, eDNA/metabarcoding, and marker choice (microsatellites, SNPs, mtDNA); analyzes with STRUCTURE/ADMIXTURE, hierfstat FST, DADA2 pipelines, and ddPCR; treats null alleles, Wahlund effect, batch effects, and eDNA allelic dropout as first-class failure modes. ([stanfish06/skillquarium](https://github.com/stanfish06/skillquarium/tree/HEAD/skills/molecular-ecologist) · ★13)
- **molecular-ecologist**: Reasons from population genetics, eDNA/metabarcoding, and marker choice (microsatellites, SNPs, mtDNA); analyzes with STRUCTURE/ADMIXTURE, hierfstat FST, DADA2 pipelines, and ddPCR; treats null alleles, Wahlund effect, batch effects, and eDNA allelic dropout as first-class failure modes. ([raktim-mondol/hermes-plugin-researchcraft](https://github.com/raktim-mondol/hermes-plugin-researchcraft/tree/HEAD/skills/molecular-ecologist) · ★5 · 3 copies)
- **molecular-ecologist**: Expert instructions and domain workflows for molecular ecologist. ([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/engineering/molecular-ecologist))
- **molecular-ecology**: Use when targeting Molecular Ecology (Mol Ecol) or deciding whether a manuscript applying molecular approaches to ecological, evolutionary, or conservation questions fits this venue. Encodes the journal's fit, framing, method-and-evidence bar, house style, official-submission re-check, and desk-reject heuristics. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/English-NaturalScience-Journal-Skills/skills/molecular-ecology) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **molecular-embedding-benchmark-eval**: Evaluates the quality of pretrained molecular representation learning models on downstream ADMET prediction tasks. It probes whether modern deep learning architectures (GNNs, transformers) can outperform traditional chemical fingerprints and established baselines like ECFP. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Collection of ADMET endpoint datasets, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Mean AUROC. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/molecular-embedding-benchmark-eval) · ★3)
- **molecular-embedding-finetuning**: Use when you have a pre-trained ROASMI checkpoint and a new user-defined retention reference set, and need to retrain the model and evaluate how far its rank-score gains transfer to new chemical and chromatographic spaces. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-embedding-finetuning))
- **molecular-embedding-generation**: Use when when you have molecular structures (SMILES or explicit graph representations) and need fixed-size vector representations to feed into a prediction head or transfer learning task. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-embedding-generation))
- **molecular-embedding-inference**: Use when you have a set of molecules (as SMILES strings in CSV format) and a pre-trained NaFM checkpoint, and you need to generate embeddings or predictions for virtual screening, bioactivity regression, or compound ranking tasks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-embedding-inference))
- **molecular-embedding-prediction**: Use when you have MSP-format MS/MS spectra (positive or negative ionization) and need to convert them into molecular embeddings to rank candidate structures, e.g. for metabolite identification or annotation of unannotated spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-embedding-prediction))
- **molecular-family-activity-scoring**: Use when you have GNPS molecular-family groupings of (possibly unknown) metabolites and want to identify whole MFs significantly changing (e.g. p&lt;=0.05, &gt;=10 molecules) between defined case/control sample groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-family-activity-scoring))
- **molecular-family-annotation-against-mibig**: Use when an LC-MS/MS molecular network contains unannotated connected components whose members should be tied to known natural product classes via MIBiG reference accessions (e.g. BGC0001840, BGC0001878, BGC0001841) and strain-level co-occurrence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-family-annotation-against-mibig))
- **molecular-family-annotation-interpretation**: Use when a feature quantification table plus MGF MS/MS spectra must be grouped into molecular families (e.g. GNPS FBMN) and scored per phenotype to identify differentially enriched, annotated families. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-family-annotation-interpretation))
- **molecular-family-class-aggregation**: Use when a GNPS molecular networking spectra.mgf file and molecular_families.tsv are available and per-spectrum CANOPUS ClassyFire/NPClassifier predictions must be aggregated into MF-level class annotations for NPClassScore linking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-family-class-aggregation))
- **molecular-family-graph-construction**: Use when you have downloaded and extracted a GNPS archive (from METABOLOMICS-SNETS, METABOLOMICS-SNETS-V2, FEATURE-BASED-MOLECULAR-NETWORKING for GNPS1, or classical_networking_workflow/feature_based_molecular_networking_workflow for GNPS2) and need to construct a queryable molecular family graph. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-family-graph-construction) · 2 copies)
- **molecular-family-grouping-analysis**: Use when you have untargeted metabolomics peak intensity data and spectral groupings (Molecular Families or Mass2Motifs) but lack confident chemical annotations or want to avoid pathway database dependency. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-family-grouping-analysis) · 3 copies)
- **molecular-family-metabolite-set-analysis**: Use when peak intensity data from an untargeted metabolomics experiment must be ranked as grouped metabolite sets (Molecular Families, Mass2Motifs) by differential activity between experimental factors, e.g. Rhamnus vs Ziziphus. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-family-metabolite-set-analysis))
- **molecular-family-motif-mapping**: Use when you have a GNPS classical or feature-based molecular network plus MS2LDA Mass2Motifs and want to know which motifs dominate each molecular family. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-family-motif-mapping))
- **molecular-family-spectra-preprocessing**: Use when raw MS/MS peak lists (e.g., from a GNPS2/FBMN task's specs_ms.mgf, keyed by scan number) must be cleaned and reduced before computing a global multiple-spectrum alignment of a molecular family. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-family-spectra-preprocessing))
- **molecular-family-substructure-consistency-evaluation**: Use when a GNPS molecular network family has mapped MS2LDA Mass2Motifs (e.g., xenoamicin ring A, putative ring B, peptidic tail motifs) and you must verify substructure-membership breakdown and &gt;75% fragment consistency against DEREPLICATOR annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-family-substructure-consistency-evaluation))
- **molecular-feature-abundance-profiling-across-fractions**: Use when a bioassay-annotated feature quantification table (row ID, row m/z, row retention time, peak area, plus bioassay values per sample) exported from MZmine2/Optimus is available and each feature's abundance across fractions must be correlated with per-fraction bioassay results. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-feature-abundance-profiling-across-fractions))
- **molecular-feature-engineering**: Use when you have a set of chemical structures (small molecules, metabolites, or drug-like compounds) represented as SMILES, SDF, mol, InChI, or mol2 files, and you need to train a machine learning model to predict a molecular property (e.g., retention time, solubility, binding affinity). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-feature-engineering))
- **molecular-feature-tracking-across-studies**: Use when a differentially abundant molecular feature from a mouse-model untargeted metabolomics study (e.g. a microbially-modulated carnitine in AD serum) needs to be matched to public human or animal metabolomics datasets to assess tissue, organism, and disease associations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-feature-tracking-across-studies))
- **molecular-featurization**: This skill covers molecular featurization for chemistry and molecular machine learning: parsing SMILES, SMARTS, InChI, SDF, and MOL2-like records; RDKit sanitization and standardization; fingerprints such as Morgan/ECFP, MACCS, atom-pair, torsion, pharmacophore, and topological fingerprints; physicochemical descriptors; 2D versus 3D descriptors; conformer-dependent features; descriptor scaling, mi ([SFETNI/Deep-Matter-Chem-Skills](https://github.com/SFETNI/Deep-Matter-Chem-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cheminformatics/molecular-featurization) · ★11)
- **molecular-fingerprint-baseline-benchmarking**: Use when a new feature set for metabolite-protein interaction prediction (e.g., shadow prices) must be benchmarked against a molecular-structure baseline under identical classifier, sampling, and cross-validation settings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-baseline-benchmarking))
- **molecular-fingerprint-bit-frequency-analysis**: Use when you have loaded a collection of molecular fingerprint vectors (e.g., from biosynfoni fingerprints deposited in Zenodo) and need to assess their statistical properties before using them for classification, similarity search, or method validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-bit-frequency-analysis) · 2 copies)
- **molecular-fingerprint-bitvector-representation**: Use when an unidentified analyte's GC/EI-MS spectrum matches no known reference (NIST/Wiley/SWGDRUG) and a structural representation must be derived from the spectrum alone to rank enumerated derivative candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-bitvector-representation))
- **molecular-fingerprint-calculation**: Use when you have a collection of small molecules in standardized format (SMILES or SDF) that require quantitative chemical feature representation for machine learning or comparative analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-calculation))
- **molecular-fingerprint-calculation-with-openbabel**: Use when candidate compounds have been retrieved (e.g. via PubChem or MetaboQuest queries) and each candidate needs a fixed-length molecular fingerprint vector to compare with a predicted fingerprint. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-calculation-with-openbabel))
- **molecular-fingerprint-computation**: Use when you have natural product molecules (or compounds from natural product-like databases such as COCONUT or ZINC) in structural format (SMILES, InChI, or SDF file) and need a fingerprint representation optimized for biosynthetic-class prediction, structural clustering, or bioinformatic feature. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/molecular-fingerprint-computation) · 2 copies)
- **molecular-fingerprint-descriptor-generation**: Use when compiling training targets for MS/MS-to-fingerprint models from .msp reference libraries (e.g., MoNA/GNPS positive and negative mode spectra) whose blocks carry InChI, SMILES, or explicit Fingerprint rows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-descriptor-generation))
- **molecular-fingerprint-distance-computation**: Use when you have per-feature fingerprint vectors (e.g., a CSI:FingerID artifact with 2936 property probabilities) and need a chemical hierarchy to enable tree-informed comparisons of untargeted metabolomics data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-distance-computation))
- **molecular-fingerprint-encoding**: Use when training or evaluating a machine-learning model that predicts compound fingerprints from MS/MS spectra, or when candidate compounds must be ranked by fingerprint similarity after spectral prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-encoding--3618fd45403418c9))
- **molecular-fingerprint-encoding**: Use when you have SMILES strings of natural product molecules and need NP-relevant structural class labels (pathway, superclass, class) predicted programmatically, or when testing whether specific structural moieties drive a classifier's predictions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-encoding--7b81bc09ce5106ca))
- **molecular-fingerprint-extraction-and-vectorization**: Use when you have annotated metabolite structures (with SMILES strings) from a reference library (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-extraction-and-vectorization) · 3 copies)
- **molecular-fingerprint-generation**: Use when small-molecule structures (SMILES, SDF, mol, mol2, hin, InChI-referenced CSVs) must be turned into fingerprint/descriptor feature matrices to train or apply ML regressors, e.g. for retention-time prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-generation--0631ca83463553cd))
- **molecular-fingerprint-generation**: Use when a generated list of candidate structures must be ranked or evaluated by structural similarity to a known reference molecule (e.g., suspect vs. anchor) using Tanimoto similarity on molecular fingerprints. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-generation--b9de3d6eaf1f3370))
- **molecular-fingerprint-generation**: Use when when you have annotated chemical structures (SMILES or InChI strings) from a curated MS/MS dataset and need to compute pairwise structural similarity scores (Tanimoto or other metrics) as training labels, or when preparing molecular representations for comparison against mass spectral data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-generation) · 2 copies)
- **molecular-fingerprint-generation**: Use when expanding a reaction network with Pickaxe and compounds must be pruned or sampled by chemical similarity to a set of target molecules before the next expansion generation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-generation--3599ba5284c7631f))
- **molecular-fingerprint-generation-and-encoding**: Use when when you have paired tandem MS spectra and corresponding molecular structures (as SMILES strings or InChI keys) and need to train a model that jointly embeds spectra and structures for structure annotation by database lookup. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-generation-and-encoding) · 2 copies)
- **molecular-fingerprint-generation-rdk**: Use when you have a set of chemical compounds represented as InChI or SMILES strings and need to extract molecular features for input into a neural network or traditional ML model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-generation-rdk))
- **molecular-fingerprint-parsing**: Use when you have received a JSON response from the CSI:FingerID web service endpoint after submitting a fragmentation tree or tandem mass spectrum query, and you need to extract the predicted molecular fingerprint representation and associated scoring metrics for compound identification or CANOPUS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-parsing) · 3 copies)
- **molecular-fingerprint-precomputation**: Use when building substructure or similarity search over a large chemical database in a store without native chemistry support (e.g. MongoDB), where per-query on-the-fly fingerprinting would be too slow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-precomputation))
- **molecular-fingerprint-prediction**: Use when you have tandem MS/MS spectra paired with known molecular structures (for training) or unknown spectra requiring structure identification, and you want to predict dense molecular fingerprint vectors that encode structural similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-prediction) · 2 copies)
- **molecular-fingerprint-prediction**: Use when untargeted LC-MS/MS feature detection (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-prediction))
- **molecular-fingerprint-prediction-from-spectra**: Use when an unidentified analyte's GC/EI-MS spectrum has no match in reference libraries (NIST, Wiley, SWGDRUG) and you need to compute its molecular fingerprint without a known chemical structure to rank candidate NPS identities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-prediction-from-spectra))
- **molecular-fingerprint-representation**: Use when building or reusing a GNN classifier that predicts reaction relationships between metabolite pairs from molecular structures, requiring fingerprint and reaction-class features for metabolic reaction network curation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-representation))
- **molecular-fingerprint-representation-learning**: Use when when you have labeled mass-spectrometry spectral data (precursor m/z and fragment m/z–intensity pairs) paired with known molecular structures (as InChIKeys or SMILES), and need to perform metabolite annotation by ranking candidate compounds based on spectral similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-representation-learning) · 2 copies)
- **molecular-fingerprint-retrieval**: Use when a metabolomics network analysis requires structural similarity links between compounds — e.g. after translating metabolite names to PubChem CIDs and needing binary substructure fingerprint bit vectors for Tanimoto-based edge weights. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-retrieval))
- **molecular-fingerprint-similarity-computation**: Decision support for choosing and computing structural fingerprints when building weighted molecular graphs from MetFrag candidate lists, or when testing fingerprint-choice sensitivity in annotation propagation benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-similarity-computation))
- **molecular-fingerprint-structural-similarity-tanimoto**: Use when when evaluating how well mass spectral similarity scores correlate with actual chemical structure for annotated spectral pairs (e.g., spectra with InChIKey metadata). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-structural-similarity-tanimoto) · 3 copies)
- **molecular-fingerprint-tanimoto-computation**: Use when you have top-matched compound pairs (query SMILES vs. library-hit SMILES) from FBMN-style annotations and need to verify that library matches are identical or structurally close to the analyzed compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-tanimoto-computation))
- **molecular-fingerprint-tanimoto-label-generation**: Use when building training data for ML models that predict chemical structural similarity from MS/MS spectra pairs, and each spectrum's compound has (or can be resolved to) an InChIKey/SMILES so a fingerprint can be computed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-tanimoto-label-generation))
- **molecular-fingerprint-tanimoto-scoring**: Use when annotated spectra (SMILES/InChI per spectrum) must be converted into pairwise structural-similarity ground truth, e.g. labelling compound pairs as related (Tanimoto &gt; 0.6) to evaluate a spectral similarity measure by precision/recall over a threshold sweep. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-tanimoto-scoring))
- **molecular-fingerprint-tanimoto-similarity**: Use when BGC- or pathway-based distance metrics must be validated or weight-optimized against a ground-truth chemical-structure distance matrix derived from reference compound SMILES (e.g., MIBiG). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-tanimoto-similarity))
- **molecular-fingerprint-tanimoto-similarity-scoring**: Use when comparing a predicted molecular structure (e.g. from de novo MS2 spectrum prediction) against a known reference structure and you need a graded similarity score or thresholded match counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-tanimoto-similarity-scoring))
- **molecular-fingerprint-tokenization**: Use when pretraining or fine-tuning a sequence model over fingerprint–molecule pairs and you need Morgan fingerprint bits (from RDKit or MIST predictions) tokenized into a unified vocabulary with SELFIES strings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-tokenization))
- **molecular-fingerprint-vector-assembly**: Use when preparing compound fingerprint labels for machine-learning training or scoring fingerprint similarity between a predicted fingerprint and database candidate structures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-fingerprint-vector-assembly))
- **molecular-fingerprints**: Molecular fingerprint generation for ML. Use when encoding molecules as feature vectors for machine learning models. ([honeyvig/skillsbench](https://github.com/honeyvig/skillsbench/tree/HEAD/shared_workspace/molecular-property-prediction/environment/skills/molecular-fingerprints))
- **molecular-format-conversion**: Convert between molecular formats including SMILES, InChI, InChIKey, and SELFIES for cheminformatics applications. ([SpectrAI-Initiative/InnoClaw](https://github.com/SpectrAI-Initiative/InnoClaw/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/molecular-format-conversion) · ★399 · 4 copies)
- **molecular-format-conversion**: Use when when ingesting raw chemical structure data from multiple external sources (publications, databases, contributor submissions) that use different molecular file formats (SMILES strings, SDF files, or other representations), and you need to unify them into a single canonical format before. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-format-conversion))
- **molecular-formula-annotation-benchmarking**: Use when comparing runtime or identification performance of molecular formula annotation tools (e.g. SIRIUS 3 vs SIRIUS 4) on fixed LC-MS/MS compound datasets with defined element alphabets (CHNOPS, BrClFI) and MS input modes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-annotation-benchmarking))
- **molecular-formula-annotation-lc-hrms**: Use when you have IDSL.IPA peaklists (mzXML/mzML/netCDF-derived m/z-RT peaks) from untargeted LC/HRMS data and need MS1-level molecular formula assignments ranked against a chemical-space database such as IDSL.ExposomeDB. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-annotation-lc-hrms))
- **molecular-formula-assignment**: Use when you have acquired MS/MS spectra (in MGF format with required fields: TITLE, PRECURSOR_MZ, PRECURSOR_TYPE, COLLISION_ENERGY) from known or unknown compounds and need to predict their molecular formulas with ranked candidates and confidence scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-formula-assignment) · 2 copies)
- **molecular-formula-assignment-and-validation**: Use when you have a calibrated FT-ICR transient (ESI_NEG or similar ionization mode) and need to annotate each detected m/z peak with its most likely elemental composition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-formula-assignment-and-validation) · 3 copies)
- **molecular-formula-assignment-comparison**: Use when comparing top-1 molecular formula assignment counts between two SIRIUS versions or configurations (isotope-pattern-only vs MS1+MS/MS) on a fixed compound set, or when recomputing reported deltas such as +6.3 pp and wrong-formula counts like 792 vs 541. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-assignment-comparison))
- **molecular-formula-assignment-count-visualization**: Use when you have a formula-assigned, filtered FT-ICR MS peak table and need per-sample QC summaries of peak counts, formula assignments, and ppm assignment errors, as done in MetaboDirect's data diagnostics step. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-assignment-count-visualization))
- **molecular-formula-assignment-from-accurate-mass**: Use when LC-HRMS feature lists with accurate m/z and MS/MS peak lists exist and each feature needs ranked chemical formula candidates before compound annotation in patRoon. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-assignment-from-accurate-mass))
- **molecular-formula-assignment-mass-spectrometry**: Use when a two-column (ion mass, intensity) peak list from UHR-MS (Orbitrap/FT-ICR) of natural organic matter or aerosol needs chemically reasonable, low-ambiguity molecular formula assignments with C, H, O and optional heteroatoms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-assignment-mass-spectrometry))
- **molecular-formula-assignment-spectra**: Use when a replicated metabolomics workflow has an MZmine3 feature table with representative MS/MS spectra and you need per-feature molecular formula and positive-ion adduct assignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-assignment-spectra))
- **molecular-formula-calculation**: Use when you have user-specified lipid class constraints (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-formula-calculation) · 2 copies)
- **molecular-formula-candidate-reranking**: Use when you have SIRIUS top-50 molecular formula candidate lists for an untargeted LC-MS/MS dataset and per-compound top-1 accuracy is insufficient (large compounds &gt;500 Da, complex extracts), and you want dataset-aware Bayesian reranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-candidate-reranking))
- **molecular-formula-candidate-retrieval**: Use when assigning molecular formulas to MS/MS precursor peaks from untargeted LC-MS data without reference spectra, and you need candidate structures from PubChem filtered by mass deviation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-candidate-retrieval))
- **molecular-formula-computation-from-structure**: Use when processing tandem MS/MS libraries in mgf format (such as GNPS) that lack a Molecular Formula (MF) field but contain valid SMILES strings. The computed formulas are required before combining libraries or writing them to msp format for MS-DIAL compatibility. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-formula-computation-from-structure) · 2 copies)
- **molecular-formula-constraint-filtering**: Use when preparing low-resolution MSn peak lists from a miniaturized mass spectrometer for fragment tree construction and you must prune candidate molecular formulas by element kinds and weight before tree building. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-constraint-filtering))
- **molecular-formula-database-filtering**: Use when when you need to constrain a large metabolite database to a specific instrumental range (e.g., m/z 100–1000) before generating virtual chemical mixtures for LC-MS/MS simulation, or when you need to verify that a reported filtered database count can be reproduced from raw database files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-formula-database-filtering) · 3 copies)
- **molecular-formula-derived-descriptor-calculation**: Use when you have a filtered FT-ICR MS peak/formula table with per-sample abundances and assigned molecular formulas, and need derived thermodynamic/structural descriptors to plot Van Krevelen diagrams, violin plots of NOSC/GFE/AImod/DBE, or compare sample groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-derived-descriptor-calculation))
- **molecular-formula-determination-isotope-fragmentation**: Use when high-resolution precursor MS/MS data (mzML/mzXML) must be assigned elemental formulas without any spectral or structure database, prior to in silico structure candidate ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-determination-isotope-fragmentation))
- **molecular-formula-distance-reranking**: Use when you have multiple molecular candidates sampled from a generative model and need to rank them by how close their molecular formula is to the reference formula, using log-probability as a tie-breaker. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-distance-reranking))
- **molecular-formula-elemental-filtering**: Use when preparing curated benchmark MS/MS datasets from HMDB, GNPS, or CASMI spectra and you must qualify entries by elemental composition, mass, precursor type, and peak count. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-elemental-filtering))
- **molecular-formula-enumeration**: Use when building a custom METASPACE molecular database for annotating matrix-cluster signals in MALDI-imaging data, i.e. when you need to systematically enumerate all plausible cluster ion formulas for a matrix such as DHB. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-enumeration--d9adb47a5335a7c3))
- **molecular-formula-enumeration**: Use when building a formula source or in-silico isotope-profile database for MS1 molecular formula annotation and you must bound the candidate formula space (organic exposome/metabolome composition) to a tractable set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-enumeration--9047fc5286cb1b09))
- **molecular-formula-gibbs-sampling**: Use when you have SIRIUS formula candidate scores (top ~50 per compound) for a whole LC-MS/MS sample and need de novo, database-independent formula reranking, especially for high-mass compounds (&gt;500 Da). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-gibbs-sampling))
- **molecular-formula-ground-truth-comparison**: Use when a benchmark provides known molecular formulas for challenges (e.g., CASMI category 1) and you need to verify that the top-ranked formula from your annotation pipeline matches the ground truth for each challenge. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-ground-truth-comparison))
- **molecular-formula-heteroatom-classification**: Use when after molecular formula assignment has been completed on detected peaks in a processed mass spectrum object. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-formula-heteroatom-classification) · 2 copies)
- **molecular-formula-hill-notation-normalization**: Use when a list of de novo molecular formula annotations (e.g. from ZODIAC on LC-MS/MS data) must be checked against a structure database and formula strings may come in inconsistent element orderings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-hill-notation-normalization))
- **molecular-formula-identification-from-tandem-msms**: Use when you have MS/MS spectra (MGF with title, precursor_mz, precursor_type, collision_energy) from Q-TOF or Orbitrap instruments and need top-1/top-5 chemical formula predictions, or must benchmark formula ID accuracy against SIRIUS and BUDDY. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-identification-from-tandem-msms))
- **molecular-formula-inference-from-adducts**: Use when when you have grouped features consolidated into empirical compounds (EmpCpds) with inferred adduct assignments from khipu, and need to perform MS1-level annotation by matching neutral formulas against JMS-compliant reference libraries (HMDB, LMSD). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-formula-inference-from-adducts) · 2 copies)
- **molecular-formula-isotope-matching**: Use when a feature's formula annotation (e.g., a novel ZODIAC/SIRIUS formula like C24H47BrNO8P at m/z 588.230) needs independent confirmation from its MS1 isotope envelope, especially for halogen-containing formulas. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-isotope-matching))
- **molecular-formula-isotopic-carbon-removal**: Use when processing peak abundance tables with assigned molecular formulas from direct-injection FT-ICR MS (e.g. a MetaboDirect-style Report.csv) where 13C isotopologue peaks must be removed before normalization and analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-isotopic-carbon-removal))
- **molecular-formula-isotopic-profile-matching**: Use when you have MS1-level LC/HRMS data (mzXML, mzML, netCDF) with chromatographic peaks and need high-confidence molecular formula annotations via isotopic profile matching rather than database-dependent MS2 search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-isotopic-profile-matching))
- **molecular-formula-isotopic-profile-matching**: Use when you have detected peaks from untargeted LC/HRMS analysis (via IDSL.IPA or equivalent peak picker) with m/z and retention time values, and you need to assign molecular formulas to those peaks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-formula-isotopic-profile-matching) · 2 copies)
- **molecular-formula-isotopologue-profile-calculation**: Use when you need to build or extend an isotopic profile database (IPDB) from a chemical list — e.g. a PubChem/CompTox/RefMet-style formula source — so that LC/HRMS MS1 peaks can be annotated with molecular formulas via theoretical-vs-experimental isotopic profile matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-isotopologue-profile-calculation))
- **molecular-formula-mass-calculation**: Use when you have a list of small-molecule molecular formulae and need theoretical monoisotopic masses plus isotopologue patterns for primary ions and adducts to drive targeted m/z trace searching in LC-IM-MS data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-mass-calculation))
- **molecular-formula-mass-matching**: Use when merging or comparing DNA adduct databases whose entries use inconsistent nomenclature — harmonize identities by chemical formula and monoisotopic mass before computing overlaps or inclusion/exclusion decisions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-mass-matching))
- **molecular-formula-monoisotopic-mass-computation**: Use when fragmenting a molecular graph into a fragmentation DAG (depth d in {3,4}) and you need per-node formula masses with hydrogen tolerance j=4 to measure total peak-intensity coverage of NIST20 MS/MS spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-monoisotopic-mass-computation))
- **molecular-formula-parsing**: Use when the input is a formula-annotated aligned peak table (output of IDSL.UFA) whose formula strings must be decomposed into element counts (C, H, Br, Cl, F, I, …) before class or progression analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-parsing))
- **molecular-formula-parsing-and-validation**: Use when you have received a formula-assigned FT-ICR MS dataset (CSV or tab-delimited table containing molecular formulas and mass values) and need to convert those formula strings into quantified elemental compositions before computing molecular descriptors, diversity indices, or transformation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-formula-parsing-and-validation) · 2 copies)
- **molecular-formula-parsing-from-smiles**: Use when you have a query MS/MS spectrum with a SMILES string and adduct type (e.g., '[M+H]+', '[M+Na]+'), and you need to validate fragment ions against chemically plausible losses from the parent compound. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-formula-parsing-from-smiles) · 2 copies)
- **molecular-formula-prediction**: Use when you have 1D ¹H or ¹³C NMR spectra from an unknown organic compound and need to predict its molecular formula (e.g., C₆H₁₂O₂). The compound must contain ≤19 heavy (non-hydrogen) atoms. Use this as the first stage of a structure elucidation pipeline before predicting molecular connectivity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-formula-prediction) · 2 copies)
- **molecular-formula-prediction-from-fragmentation**: Use when you have MS/MS fragmentation spectra (from Orbitrap or Q-TOF instruments) in MGF format with known precursor m/z, adduct type, and collision energy, and you need to generate ranked molecular formula candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-formula-prediction-from-fragmentation) · 3 copies)
- **molecular-formula-prediction-sirius**: Use when post-processing pre-processed featureXML/mzML files from OpenMS (UmetaFlow) — each feature's MS2 spectrum plus its adduct/ionisation-mode information from config.yaml — to rank molecular formula candidates and record the top-ranked SIRIUS formula per feature. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-prediction-sirius))
- **molecular-formula-property-derivation**: Use when you have filtered FT-ICR MS peak data with valid molecular formula assignments (C, H, O, N, S, P elemental counts) and need to quantify molecular properties that predict metabolite reactivity, bioavailability, and biochemical role. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-formula-property-derivation) · 2 copies)
- **molecular-formula-quality-assessment**: Use when immediately after formula assignment from raw FT-ICR MS peak detection, when you have a peak intensity matrix with assigned molecular formulas and need to remove spurious or low-confidence assignments before calculating thermodynamic indices, determining compound classes, or performing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-formula-quality-assessment) · 2 copies)
- **molecular-formula-rank-aggregation**: Use when IDSL.IPA has generated individual per-sample peaklists and an aligned peak table from untargeted LC/HRMS (mzXML/mzML/netCDF) and you need to consolidate formula annotations across samples into a summary table ranked by frequency and median rank. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-rank-aggregation))
- **molecular-formula-representation-and-manipulation**: Use when you need to represent, validate, and manipulate molecular compositions in MS analysis—specifically when annotating precursor or product ions with elemental formulas, computing monoisotopic masses for formula-to-charge assignments, predicting isotope patterns ([M]+, [M+H]+, [M+Na]+, etc.). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-formula-representation-and-manipulation) · 2 copies)
- **molecular-formula-reranking-zodiac**: Use when features from an untargeted LC-MS/MS run have candidate formulas ranked by SIRIUS fragmentation trees but top-ranked candidates may be wrong due to interference, and before fingerprint prediction via CSI:FingerID. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-reranking-zodiac))
- **molecular-formula-scoring-and-ranking**: Use when IDSL.IPA per-sample peak lists must be annotated with molecular formulas and surviving candidate formulas (post PCS/NEME/NDCS/RCS filters) need a composite match score and descending rank order, per sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-scoring-and-ranking))
- **molecular-formula-search-assignment**: Use when you have a recalibrated FT-ICR mass spectrum (Bruker .d format or equivalent) with detected, noise-thresholded peaks and need to assign chemical formulas to each peak. This is particularly relevant when analyzing samples with unknown composition (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-formula-search-assignment) · 2 copies)
- **molecular-formula-stratified-grouping**: Use when training ESP's ensemble classifier on spectra for which ground-truth molecular formulae are known, and you need per-molecule candidate groups drawn from the training data itself (e.g. NPLIB1/NIST-derived spectra in torch_trvate_10bin.pkl). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-stratified-grouping))
- **molecular-formula-subset-search**: Use when assigning candidate molecular formulas to LC-MS/MS metabolite spectra (e.g., mzML from QTOF/ITFT/QFT/Orbitrap datasets) without spectral-library matches, or as a fast prescreening step before SIRIUS/Buddy-style identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/molecular-formula-subset-search))
- **molecular-formula-to-carbon-count-extraction**: Use when when you have a feature list from HRMS data with molecular formula annotations (inferred or assigned) and need to compute per-carbon mass defect ratios (MD/C, m/C) as part of PFAS candidate prioritization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-formula-to-carbon-count-extraction) · 2 copies)
- **molecular-formula-validation**: Use when when you have a feature list from HRMS with tentatively assigned molecular formulas (from in silico tools or databases) and need to assess formula plausibility before applying downstream PFAS-specific filters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/molecular-formula-validation) · 3 copies)
